  var SVG_NS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
  var FRAME_PART = 'data-vis-frame';
  var DEFAULT_MARGIN = Object.freeze({ top: 12, right: 12, bottom: 28, left: 40 });
  var GRID_PAD = 8;
  var BAND_PADDING = 0.3;
  var BAR_MAX = 32;
  var BAR_GAP = 4;
  var AXIS_LABEL_SPACE = 18;

  function requireD3() {
    if (typeof window.d3 === 'undefined') {
      throw new Error('d3 is not loaded. Add the D3 runtime marker (a script tag with data-runtime="d3") to the fragment once.');
    }
    return window.d3;
  }

  function isBand(scale) {
    return scale !== undefined && scale !== null && typeof scale.bandwidth === 'function';
  }

  function compact(value) {
    var abs = Math.abs(value);
    if (abs < 1000) return new Intl.NumberFormat(undefined, { maximumFractionDigits: 2 }).format(value);
    return new Intl.NumberFormat(undefined, { notation: 'compact', maximumFractionDigits: 1 })
      .format(value)
      .toLowerCase();
  }

  function measureCanvas() {
    var canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    var context = canvas.getContext('2d');
    var font = getComputedStyle(document.body || document.documentElement).fontFamily;
    if (context) context.font = '11px ' + font;
    return context;
  }

  function labelMargin(labels) {
    var context = measureCanvas();
    var widest = 0;
    (labels || []).forEach(function (label) {
      var text = String(label);
      var width = context ? context.measureText(text).width : text.length * 6.5;
      if (width > widest) widest = width;
    });
    return Math.min(240, Math.max(DEFAULT_MARGIN.left, Math.ceil(widest) + GRID_PAD * 2));
  }

  function band(domain, range) {
    var d3 = requireD3();
    return d3
      .scaleBand()
      .domain(domain)
      .range(range || [0, 1])
      .paddingInner(BAND_PADDING)
      .paddingOuter(BAND_PADDING / 2);
  }

  function draw(node, paint) {
    var last = '';
    var run = function () {
      var rect = node.getBoundingClientRect();
      var width = Math.floor(rect.width);
      var height = Math.floor(rect.height);
      if (width < 1 || height < 1) return;
      var key = width + 'x' + height;
      if (key === last) return;
      last = key;
      paint({ width: width, height: height });
    };
    new ResizeObserver(run).observe(node);
    run();
  }

  function svgElement(parent, tag, className) {
    var element = document.createElementNS(SVG_NS, tag);
    if (className) element.setAttribute('class', className);
    parent.appendChild(element);
    return element;
  }

  function tickCount(span, spacing) {
    return Math.max(2, Math.floor(span / spacing));
  }

  function axisFormat(scale, format) {
    if (typeof format === 'function') return format;
    if (typeof format === 'string') return requireD3().format(format);
    if (isBand(scale) || typeof scale.ticks !== 'function') return null;
    if (typeof scale.invert === 'function' && scale.domain()[0] instanceof Date) return null;
    return compact;
  }

  function clearFrame(node) {
    for (var i = node.children.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
      if (node.children[i].hasAttribute(FRAME_PART)) node.removeChild(node.children[i]);
    }
  }

  function frame(node, options) {
    var d3 = requireD3();
    var o = options || {};
    clearFrame(node);
    var rect = node.getBoundingClientRect();
    var width = o.width !== undefined ? o.width : rect.width;
    var height = o.height !== undefined ? o.height : rect.height;
    var margin = Object.assign({}, DEFAULT_MARGIN, o.margin || {}, o.margins || {});
    if (o.xLabel) margin.bottom += AXIS_LABEL_SPACE;
    if (o.yLabel) margin.left += AXIS_LABEL_SPACE;
    var x0 = margin.left;
    var x1 = width - margin.right;
    var y0 = margin.top;
    var y1 = height - margin.bottom;
    if (x1 - x0 < 24 || y1 - y0 < 24) return null;
    var x = o.x;
    var y = o.y;
    if (x) x.range(o.xRange || [x0, x1]);
    if (y) y.range(o.yRange || (isBand(y) ? [y0, y1] : [y1, y0]));
    var svg = d3.select(node);
    var showXGrid = o.xGrid !== undefined ? o.xGrid : Boolean(x && !isBand(x) && y && isBand(y));
    var showYGrid = o.yGrid !== undefined ? o.yGrid : Boolean(y && !isBand(y));
    if (showYGrid && y && typeof y.ticks === 'function') {
      var yTicks = o.yTickValues || y.ticks(o.yTicks || tickCount(y1 - y0, 40));
      svg
        .append('g')
        .attr('class', 'vis-grid-y')
        .attr(FRAME_PART, '')
        .selectAll('line')
        .data(yTicks)
        .join('line')
        .attr('x1', x0)
        .attr('x2', x1)
        .attr('y1', function (d) { return y(d); })
        .attr('y2', function (d) { return y(d); });
    }
    if (showXGrid && x && typeof x.ticks === 'function') {
      var xTicks = o.xTickValues || x.ticks(o.xTicks || tickCount(x1 - x0, 80));
      svg
        .append('g')
        .attr('class', 'vis-grid-x')
        .attr(FRAME_PART, '')
        .selectAll('line')
        .data(xTicks)
        .join('line')
        .attr('y1', y0)
        .attr('y2', y1)
        .attr('x1', function (d) { return x(d); })
        .attr('x2', function (d) { return x(d); });
    }
    if (x && o.xAxis !== false) {
      var bottom = d3.axisBottom(x).tickSizeOuter(0).tickSize(4).tickPadding(6);
      if (!isBand(x)) bottom.ticks(o.xTicks || tickCount(x1 - x0, 80));
      if (o.xTickValues) bottom.tickValues(o.xTickValues);
      var xFormat = axisFormat(x, o.xFormat);
      if (xFormat) bottom.tickFormat(xFormat);
      svg.append('g').attr('class', 'vis-axis vis-axis-x').attr(FRAME_PART, '').attr('transform', 'translate(0,' + y1 + ')').call(bottom);
    }
    if (y && o.yAxis !== false) {
      var left = d3.axisLeft(y).tickSizeOuter(0).tickSize(isBand(y) ? 0 : 4).tickPadding(6);
      if (!isBand(y)) left.ticks(o.yTicks || tickCount(y1 - y0, 40));
      if (o.yTickValues) left.tickValues(o.yTickValues);
      var yFormat = axisFormat(y, o.yFormat);
      if (yFormat) left.tickFormat(yFormat);
      var yAxis = svg.append('g').attr('class', 'vis-axis vis-axis-y').attr(FRAME_PART, '').attr('transform', 'translate(' + x0 + ',0)').call(left);
      if (!isBand(y)) yAxis.select('.domain').remove();
    }
    if (o.xLabel) {
      var xLabel = svgElement(node, 'text', 'vis-axis-label');
      xLabel.setAttribute(FRAME_PART, '');
      xLabel.setAttribute('x', String((x0 + x1) / 2));
      xLabel.setAttribute('y', String(height - 4));
      xLabel.setAttribute('text-anchor', 'middle');
      xLabel.textContent = o.xLabel;
    }
    if (o.yLabel) {
      var yLabel = svgElement(node, 'text', 'vis-axis-label');
      yLabel.setAttribute(FRAME_PART, '');
      yLabel.setAttribute('transform', 'translate(12,' + (y0 + y1) / 2 + ') rotate(-90)');
      yLabel.setAttribute('text-anchor', 'middle');
      yLabel.textContent = o.yLabel;
    }
    return {
      x: x,
      y: y,
      x0: x0,
      x1: x1,
      y0: y0,
      y1: y1,
      width: width,
      height: height,
      innerWidth: x1 - x0,
      innerHeight: y1 - y0,
      margin: margin,
    };
  }

  window.chart = Object.freeze({
    draw: draw,
    frame: frame,
    band: band,
    labelMargin: labelMargin,
    DEFAULT_MARGIN: DEFAULT_MARGIN,
    GRID_PAD: GRID_PAD,
    BAND_PADDING: BAND_PADDING,
    BAR_MAX: BAR_MAX,
    BAR_GAP: BAR_GAP,
  });

  function hydrateIcons(root) {
    var icons = root.querySelectorAll ? root.querySelectorAll('i[data-lucide]') : [];
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(icons, function (icon) {
      var name = icon.getAttribute('data-lucide');
      var body = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(ICONS, name) ? ICONS[name] : null;
      if (body === null || icon.getAttribute('data-lucide-hydrated') === name) return;
      icon.setAttribute('data-lucide-hydrated', name);
      icon.classList.add('vis-icon');
      icon.innerHTML =
        '<svg xmlns="' + SVG_NS + '" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">' +
        body +
        '</svg>';
    });
  }

  var tooltip = null;
  var tooltipOwner = null;

  function tooltipTarget(node) {
    return node && node.closest ? node.closest('[data-tooltip],[data-tooltip-label]') : null;
  }

  function showTooltip(target, clientX, clientY) {
    if (tooltip === null) {
      tooltip = document.createElement('div');
      tooltip.className = 'vis-tooltip';
      tooltip.setAttribute('role', 'tooltip');
      tooltip.hidden = true;
      document.body.appendChild(tooltip);
    }
    var label = target.getAttribute('data-tooltip-label');
    var value = target.getAttribute('data-tooltip-value');
    tooltip.textContent = '';
    if (label !== null && value !== null) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'vis-tooltip-row';
      var labelNode = document.createElement('span');
      labelNode.className = 'vis-tooltip-label';
      labelNode.textContent = label;
      var valueNode = document.createElement('span');
      valueNode.className = 'vis-tooltip-value';
      valueNode.textContent = value;
      row.appendChild(labelNode);
      row.appendChild(valueNode);
      tooltip.appendChild(row);
    } else {
      tooltip.textContent = target.getAttribute('data-tooltip') || label || '';
    }
    tooltipOwner = target;
    tooltip.hidden = false;
    var box = tooltip.getBoundingClientRect();
    var left = Math.min(clientX + 12, window.innerWidth - box.width - 4);
    var top = clientY + 12 + box.height > window.innerHeight ? clientY - box.height - 12 : clientY + 12;
    tooltip.style.left = Math.max(4, left) + 'px';
    tooltip.style.top = Math.max(4, top) + 'px';
  }

  function hideTooltip() {
    if (tooltip !== null) tooltip.hidden = true;
    tooltipOwner = null;
  }

  document.addEventListener('pointermove', function (event) {
    var target = tooltipTarget(event.target);
    if (target === null) {
      if (tooltipOwner !== null) hideTooltip();
      return;
    }
    showTooltip(target, event.clientX, event.clientY);
  });
  document.addEventListener('pointerleave', hideTooltip);
  document.addEventListener('focusin', function (event) {
    var target = tooltipTarget(event.target);
    if (target === null) return;
    var rect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
    showTooltip(target, rect.left, rect.bottom);
  });
  document.addEventListener('focusout', hideTooltip);

  function start() {
    hydrateIcons(document);
    new MutationObserver(function (records) {
      records.forEach(function (record) {
        Array.prototype.forEach.call(record.addedNodes, function (added) {
          if (added.nodeType !== 1) return;
          if (added.matches && added.matches('i[data-lucide]')) hydrateIcons(added.parentNode || document);
          else hydrateIcons(added);
        });
        if (record.type === 'attributes') hydrateIcons(record.target.parentNode || document);
      });
    }).observe(document.documentElement, {
      childList: true,
      subtree: true,
      attributes: true,
      attributeFilter: ['data-lucide'],
    });
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', start);
  else start();
